```
     1→# CrewAI Platform - Infrastructure Resources
     2→# All environment-specific values loaded from ../config.json via locals.tf
     3→# All resources defined inline (no remote module dependencies)
     4→
     5→# --- Providers ---
     6→
     7→provider "google" {
     8→  project = local.global.project_ids.gke
     9→  region  = local.global.region
    10→}
    11→
    12→provider "kubernetes" {
    13→  host                   = "https://${data.google_container_cluster.gke.endpoint}"
    14→  token                  = data.google_client_config.default.access_token
    15→  cluster_ca_certificate = base64decode(data.google_container_cluster.gke.master_auth[0].cluster_ca_certificate)
    16→}
    17→
    18→# --- Data Sources ---
    19→
    20→data "google_client_config" "default" {}
    21→
    22→data "google_container_cluster" "gke" {
    23→  name     = local.global.gke_cluster.name
    24→  location = local.global.region
    25→  project  = local.global.project_ids.gke
    26→}
    27→
    28→data "google_sql_database_instance" "platform" {
    29→  project = local.global.project_ids.data
    30→  name    = local.global.cloud_sql.instance_name
    31→}
    32→
    33→# --- Namespaces ---
    34→
    35→resource "kubernetes_namespace_v1" "crewai_platform" {
    36→  metadata {
    37→    name = local.service.namespaces.platform
    38→
    39→    labels = {
    40→      app         = "crewai"
    41→      environment = local.global.environment
    42→      managed-by  = "terraform"
    43→      platform    = "kix"
    44→    }
    45→  }
    46→
    47→  lifecycle {
    48→    ignore_changes = [metadata[0].labels]
    49→  }
    50→}
    51→
    52→resource "kubernetes_namespace_v1" "crewai_crews" {
    53→  metadata {
    54→    name = local.service.namespaces.crews
    55→
    56→    labels = {
    57→      app         = "crewai-crews"
    58→      environment = local.global.environment
    59→      managed-by  = "terraform"
    60→      platform    = "kix"
    61→    }
    62→  }
    63→
    64→  lifecycle {
    65→    ignore_changes = [metadata[0].labels]
    66→  }
    67→}
    68→
    69→# --- GCP Service Account ---
    70→
    71→resource "google_service_account" "crewai_platform" {
    72→  project      = local.global.project_ids.gke
    73→  account_id   = local.service.gcp_service_account.name
    74→  display_name = "CrewAI Platform - ${local.global.environment}"
    75→}
    76→
    77→# --- IAM Bindings ---
    78→
    79→resource "google_project_iam_member" "cloudsql_client" {
    80→  project = local.global.project_ids.data
    81→  role    = "roles/cloudsql.client"
    82→  member  = "serviceAccount:${google_service_account.crewai_platform.email}"
    83→}
    84→
    85→resource "google_project_iam_member" "cloudsql_user" {
    86→  project = local.global.project_ids.data
    87→  role    = "roles/cloudsql.instanceUser"
    88→  member  = "serviceAccount:${google_service_account.crewai_platform.email}"
    89→}
    90→
    91→resource "google_storage_bucket_iam_member" "data_bucket" {
    92→  bucket = google_storage_bucket.gcs_data.name
    93→  role   = "roles/storage.objectAdmin"
    94→  member = "serviceAccount:${google_service_account.crewai_platform.email}"
    95→}
    96→
    97→resource "google_storage_bucket_iam_member" "logs_bucket" {
    98→  bucket = google_storage_bucket.gcs_logs.name
    99→  role   = "roles/storage.objectCreator"
   100→  member = "serviceAccount:${google_service_account.crewai_platform.email}"
   101→}
   102→
   103→# TODO: Re-enable when SA has artifactregistry.repositories.getIamPolicy on kd-ix-eur-shr-artifacts
   104→# resource "google_artifact_registry_repository_iam_member" "shared_ar_reader" {
   105→#   project    = local.global.artifacts.shared_project
   106→#   location   = local.global.region
   107→#   repository = local.global.artifacts.shared_repository
   108→#   role       = "roles/artifactregistry.reader"
   109→#   member     = "serviceAccount:${google_service_account.crewai_platform.email}"
   110→# }
   111→
   112→resource "google_project_iam_member" "secret_accessor" {
   113→  project = local.global.project_ids.security
   114→  role    = "roles/secretmanager.secretAccessor"
   115→  member  = "serviceAccount:${google_service_account.crewai_platform.email}"
   116→}
   117→
   118→# --- Workload Identity ---
   119→
   120→resource "google_service_account_iam_member" "workload_identity" {
   121→  service_account_id = google_service_account.crewai_platform.name
   122→  role               = "roles/iam.workloadIdentityUser"
   123→  member             = "serviceAccount:${local.global.project_ids.gke}.svc.id.goog[${local.service.namespaces.platform}/${local.service.k8s_service_account}]"
   124→}
   125→
   126→resource "google_service_account_iam_member" "github_impersonation" {
   127→  service_account_id = google_service_account.crewai_platform.name
   128→  role               = "roles/iam.serviceAccountTokenCreator"
   129→  member             = "serviceAccount:github-crewai-${local.global.environment}@${local.global.project_ids.gke}.iam.gserviceaccount.com"
   130→}
   131→
   132→# --- Cloud SQL Databases ---
   133→
   134→resource "google_sql_database" "production" {
   135→  project  = local.global.project_ids.data
   136→  instance = data.google_sql_database_instance.platform.name
   137→  name     = local.service.database.names.production
   138→}
   139→
   140→resource "google_sql_database" "cable" {
   141→  project  = local.global.project_ids.data
   142→  instance = data.google_sql_database_instance.platform.name
   143→  name     = local.service.database.names.cable
   144→}
   145→
   146→resource "google_sql_database" "oauth" {
   147→  project  = local.global.project_ids.data
   148→  instance = data.google_sql_database_instance.platform.name
   149→  name     = local.service.database.names.oauth
   150→}
   151→
   152→# --- Cloud SQL IAM User ---
   153→# Uses IAM authentication via Workload Identity through Cloud SQL Proxy.
   154→# Requires cloudsql.instanceUser IAM binding above.
   155→
   156→resource "google_sql_user" "crewai_iam" {
   157→  project  = local.global.project_ids.data
   158→  instance = data.google_sql_database_instance.platform.name
   159→  name     = trimsuffix(google_service_account.crewai_platform.email, ".gserviceaccount.com")
   160→  type     = "CLOUD_IAM_SERVICE_ACCOUNT"
   161→}
   162→
   163→# --- GCS Buckets ---
   164→
   165→resource "google_storage_bucket" "gcs_data" {
   166→  project                     = local.global.project_ids.gke
   167→  name                        = "${local.global.project_ids.gke}-crewai-${local.service.storage.data_suffix}"
   168→  location                    = local.global.region
   169→  uniform_bucket_level_access = true
   170→  force_destroy               = false
   171→
   172→  versioning {
   173→    enabled = true
   174→  }
   175→
   176→  labels = {
   177→    app         = "crewai"
   178→    environment = local.global.environment
   179→    managed-by  = "terraform"
   180→    cost-center = local.service.storage.cost_center
   181→  }
   182→}
   183→
   184→resource "google_storage_bucket" "gcs_logs" {
   185→  project                     = local.global.project_ids.gke
   186→  name                        = "${local.global.project_ids.gke}-crewai-${local.service.storage.logs_suffix}"
   187→  location                    = local.global.region
   188→  uniform_bucket_level_access = true
   189→  force_destroy               = false
   190→
   191→  versioning {
   192→    enabled = true
   193→  }
   194→
   195→  labels = {
   196→    app         = "crewai"
   197→    environment = local.global.environment
   198→    managed-by  = "terraform"
   199→    cost-center = local.service.storage.cost_center
   200→  }
   201→}
   202→
   203→# --- HMAC Keys for S3-Compatible GCS Access ---
   204→
   205→resource "google_storage_hmac_key" "crewai" {
   206→  project               = local.global.project_ids.gke
   207→  service_account_email = google_service_account.crewai_platform.email
   208→}
   209→
   210→# --- Secret Manager: GCS Credentials ---
   211→
   212→resource "google_secret_manager_secret" "gcs_credentials" {
   213→  project   = local.global.project_ids.security
   214→  secret_id = local.service.secrets.gcs_credentials
   215→
   216→  replication {
   217→    user_managed {
   218→      replicas {
   219→        location = "europe-west1"
   220→      }
   221→      replicas {
   222→        location = "europe-west4"
   223→      }
   224→    }
   225→  }
   226→
   227→  labels = {
   228→    app         = "crewai"
   229→    environment = local.global.environment
   230→    managed-by  = "terraform"
   231→    platform    = "kix"
   232→  }
   233→}
   234→
   235→resource "google_secret_manager_secret_version" "gcs_credentials" {
   236→  secret=[REDACTED].gcs_credentials.id
   237→  secret_data = jsonencode({
   238→    access_key_id     = google_storage_hmac_key.crewai.access_id
   239→    secret_access_key = google_storage_hmac_key.crewai.secret
   240→  })
   241→}
   242→
   243→resource "google_secret_manager_secret_iam_member" "gcs_credentials_accessor" {
   244→  secret_id = google_secret_manager_secret.gcs_credentials.id
   245→  role      = "roles/secretmanager.secretAccessor"
   246→  member    = "serviceAccount:${google_service_account.crewai_platform.email}"
   247→}
   248→
   249→# --- Secret Manager: Rails Secrets ---
   250→# Auto-generated secrets for Rails (SECRET_KEY_BASE, encryption keys, internal API key).
   251→# Values are generated once by Terraform and preserved in state across applies.
   252→
   253→resource "random_password" "rails_secret_key_base" {

   257→

   350→resource "google_secret_manager_secret_version" "workos_credentials" {
   351→  secret=[REDACTED].workos_credentials.id
   352→  secret_data = jsonencode({
   353→    client_id       = var.oauth_client_id
   354→    api_key         = var.oauth_api_key
   355→    cookie_password = var.oauth_cookie_password
   356→    authkit_domain  = var.oauth_domain
   357→  })
   358→}
   359→
   360→resource "google_secret_manager_secret_iam_member" "workos_credentials_accessor" {
   361→  secret_id = google_secret_manager_secret.workos_credentials.id
   362→  role      = "roles/secretmanager.secretAccessor"
   363→  member    = "serviceAccount:${google_service_account.crewai_platform.email}"
   364→}
   365→
   366→# --- Secret Manager: GitHub App Credentials ---
   367→# GitHub App credentials for repository integration.
   368→# Values injected via TF_VAR_GITHUB_* from GitHub Secrets.
   369→
   370→resource "google_secret_manager_secret" "github_credentials" {
   371→  project   = local.global.project_ids.security
   372→  secret_id = local.service.secrets.github_credentials
   373→
   374→  replication {
   375→    user_managed {
   376→      replicas {
   377→        location = "europe-west1"
   378→      }
   379→      replicas {
   380→        location = "europe-west4"
   381→      }
   382→    }
   383→  }
   384→
   385→  labels = {
   386→    app         = "crewai"
   387→    environment = local.global.environment
   388→    managed-by  = "terraform"
   389→    platform    = "kix"
   390→  }
   391→}
   392→
   393→resource "google_secret_manager_secret_version" "github_credentials" {
   394→  secret=[REDACTED].github_credentials.id
   395→  secret_data = jsonencode({
   396→    client_secret   = var.github_client_secret
   397→    app_private_key = var.github_app_private_key
   398→    github_token    = var.github_token
   399→  })
   400→}
   401→
   402→resource "google_secret_manager_secret_iam_member" "github_credentials_accessor" {
   403→  secret_id = google_secret_manager_secret.github_credentials.id
   404→  role      = "roles/secretmanager.secretAccessor"
   405→  member    = "serviceAccount:${google_service_account.crewai_platform.email}"
   406→}
   407→
   408→# --- Secret Manager: Replicated SDK Credentials ---
   409→# Replicated SDK auth token for license validation and update checks.
   410→# Value injected via TF_VAR_REPLICATED_AUTH_TOKEN from GitHub Secrets.
   411→
   412→resource "google_secret_manager_secret" "replicated_credentials" {
   413→  project   = local.global.project_ids.security
   414→  secret_id = local.service.secrets.replicated_credentials
   415→
   416→  replication {
   417→    user_managed {
   418→      replicas {
   419→        location = "europe-west1"
   420→      }
   421→      replicas {
   422→        location = "europe-west4"
   423→      }
   424→    }
   425→  }
   426→
   427→  labels = {
   428→    app         = "crewai"
   429→    environment = local.global.environment
   430→    managed-by  = "terraform"
   431→    platform    = "kix"
   432→  }
   433→}
   434→
   435→resource "google_secret_manager_secret_version" "replicated_credentials" {
   436→  secret=[REDACTED].replicated_credentials.id
   437→  secret_data = jsonencode({
   438→    auth_token = var.replicated_auth_token
   439→  })
   440→}
   441→
   442→resource "google_secret_manager_secret_iam_member" "replicated_credentials_accessor" {
   443→  secret_id = google_secret_manager_secret.replicated_credentials.id
   444→  role      = "roles/secretmanager.secretAccessor"
   445→  member    = "serviceAccount:${google_service_account.crewai_platform.email}"
   446→}
   447→
   448→# --- Artifact Registry ---
   449→
   450→resource "google_artifact_registry_repository" "crewai_builder" {
   451→  project       = local.global.project_ids.gke
   452→  location      = local.global.region
   453→  repository_id = "crewai-${local.global.environment}"
   454→  description   = "Container registry for CrewAI crew builds"
   455→  format        = "DOCKER"
   456→
   457→  labels = {
   458→    app         = "crewai"
   459→    environment = local.global.environment
   460→    managed-by  = "terraform"
   461→    platform    = "kix"
   462→  }
   463→}
   464→
   465→resource "google_artifact_registry_repository_iam_member" "builder_writer" {
   466→  project    = local.global.project_ids.gke
   467→  location   = local.global.region
   468→  repository = google_artifact_registry_repository.crewai_builder.name
   469→  role       = "roles/artifactregistry.writer"
   470→  member     = "serviceAccount:${google_service_account.crewai_platform.email}"
   471→}
   472→
   473→resource "google_artifact_registry_repository_iam_member" "builder_reader" {
   474→  project    = local.global.project_ids.gke
   475→  location   = local.global.region
   476→  repository = google_artifact_registry_repository.crewai_builder.name
   477→  role       = "roles/artifactregistry.reader"
   478→  member     = "serviceAccount:${google_service_account.crewai_platform.email}"
   479→}
   480→
   481→# --- Certificate Manager ---
   482→
   483→resource "google_certificate_manager_certificate" "crewai_cert" {
   484→  name        = local.service.certificate.name
   485→  project     = local.global.project_ids.gke
   486→  description = "Google-managed SSL certificate for CrewAI Platform - ${local.global.environment}"
   487→  scope       = "DEFAULT"
   488→
   489→  managed {
   490→    domains = [local.service.hostname]
   491→  }
   492→
   493→  labels = {
   494→    service     = "crewai"
   495→    environment = local.global.environment
   496→    managed-by  = "terraform"
   497→  }
   498→}
   499→
   500→resource "google_certificate_manager_certificate_map" "crewai_certmap" {
   501→  name        = local.service.certificate.map
   502→  project     = local.global.project_ids.gke
   503→  description = "Certificate map for CrewAI Platform Gateway - ${local.global.environment}"
   504→
   505→  labels = {
   506→    service     = "crewai"
   507→    environment = local.global.environment
   508→    managed-by  = "terraform"
   509→  }
   510→}
   511→
   512→resource "google_certificate_manager_certificate_map_entry" "crewai_certmap_entry" {
   513→  name         = "${local.service.certificate.name}-entry"
   514→  project      = local.global.project_ids.gke
   515→  map          = google_certificate_manager_certificate_map.crewai_certmap.name
   516→  certificates = [google_certificate_manager_certificate.crewai_cert.id]
   517→  hostname     = local.service.hostname
   518→}
   519→
   520→# --- Kubernetes Service Account ---
   521→
   522→resource "kubernetes_service_account_v1" "crewai_platform" {
   523→  metadata {
   524→    name      = local.service.k8s_service_account
   525→    namespace = local.service.namespaces.platform
   526→    annotations = {
   527→      "iam.gke.io/gcp-service-account" = google_service_account.crewai_platform.email
   528→    }
   529→    labels = {
   530→      app         = "crewai"
   531→      environment = local.global.environment
   532→      managed-by  = "terraform"
   533→    }
   534→  }
   535→
   536→  depends_on = [kubernetes_namespace_v1.crewai_platform]
   537→}
   538→
   539→resource "kubernetes_service_account_v1" "crewai_crews" {
   540→  metadata {
   541→    name      = "crewai-crews-sa"
   542→    namespace = local.service.namespaces.crews
   543→    labels = {
   544→      app         = "crewai"
   545→      environment = local.global.environment
   546→      managed-by  = "terraform"
   547→    }
   548→  }
   549→
   550→  depends_on = [kubernetes_namespace_v1.crewai_crews]
   551→}
   552→
   553→# --- Kubernetes Limit Ranges ---
   554→
   555→resource "kubernetes_limit_range_v1" "crewai_platform" {
   556→  metadata {
   557→    name      = "crewai-platform-limits"
   558→    namespace = local.service.namespaces.platform
   559→    labels = {
   560→      app         = "crewai"
   561→      environment = local.global.environment
   562→      managed-by  = "terraform"
   563→    }
   564→  }
   565→  spec {
   566→    limit {
   567→      type = "Container"
   568→      default = {
   569→        cpu    = local.service.limit_ranges.platform.default_cpu
   570→        memory = local.service.limit_ranges.platform.default_memory
   571→      }
   572→      default_request = {
   573→        cpu    = local.service.limit_ranges.platform.default_request_cpu
   574→        memory = local.service.limit_ranges.platform.default_request_memory
   575→      }
   576→    }
   577→  }
   578→
   579→  depends_on = [kubernetes_namespace_v1.crewai_platform]
   580→}
   581→
   582→resource "kubernetes_limit_range_v1" "crewai_crews" {
   583→  metadata {
   584→    name      = "crewai-crews-limits"
   585→    namespace = local.service.namespaces.crews
   586→    labels = {
   587→      app         = "crewai"
   588→      environment = local.global.environment
   589→      managed-by  = "terraform"
   590→    }
   591→  }
   592→  spec {
   593→    limit {
   594→      type = "Container"
   595→      default = {
   596→        cpu    = local.service.limit_ranges.crews.default_cpu
   597→        memory = local.service.limit_ranges.crews.default_memory
   598→      }
   599→      default_request = {
   600→        cpu    = local.service.limit_ranges.crews.default_request_cpu
   601→        memory = local.service.limit_ranges.crews.default_request_memory
   602→      }
   603→    }
   604→  }
   605→
   606→  depends_on = [kubernetes_namespace_v1.crewai_crews]
   607→}
   608→
   609→# --- Kubernetes Resource Quotas ---
   610→
   611→resource "kubernetes_resource_quota_v1" "crewai_platform" {
   612→  metadata {
   613→    name      = "crewai-platform-quota"
   614→    namespace = local.service.namespaces.platform
   615→    labels = {
   616→      app         = "crewai"
   617→      environment = local.global.environment
   618→      managed-by  = "terraform"
   619→    }
   620→  }
   621→  spec {
   622→    hard = {
   623→      "requests.cpu"         = local.service.quotas.platform.requests_cpu
   624→      "requests.memory"      = local.service.quotas.platform.requests_memory
   625→      "limits.cpu"           = local.service.quotas.platform.limits_cpu
   626→      "limits.memory"        = local.service.quotas.platform.limits_memory
   627→      pods                   = local.service.quotas.platform.pods
   628→      services               = local.service.quotas.platform.services
   629→      secrets                = local.service.quotas.platform.secrets
   630→      configmaps             = local.service.quotas.platform.configmaps
   631→      persistentvolumeclaims = local.service.quotas.platform.pvcs
   632→    }
   633→  }
   634→
   635→  depends_on = [kubernetes_limit_range_v1.crewai_platform]
   636→}
   637→
   638→resource "kubernetes_resource_quota_v1" "crewai_crews" {
   639→  metadata {
   640→    name      = "crewai-crews-quota"
   641→    namespace = local.service.namespaces.crews
   642→    labels = {
   643→      app         = "crewai"
   644→      environment = local.global.environment
   645→      managed-by  = "terraform"
   646→    }
   647→  }
   648→  spec {
   649→    hard = {
   650→      "requests.cpu"         = local.service.quotas.crews.requests_cpu
   651→      "requests.memory"      = local.service.quotas.crews.requests_memory
   652→      "limits.cpu"           = local.service.quotas.crews.limits_cpu
   653→      "limits.memory"        = local.service.quotas.crews.limits_memory
   654→      pods                   = local.service.quotas.crews.pods
   655→      services               = local.service.quotas.crews.services
   656→      secrets                = local.service.quotas.crews.secrets
   657→      configmaps             = local.service.quotas.crews.configmaps
   658→      persistentvolumeclaims = local.service.quotas.crews.pvcs
   659→    }
   660→  }
   661→
   662→  depends_on = [kubernetes_limit_range_v1.crewai_crews]
   663→}
   664→
   665→# --- Cloud SQL Proxy - Standalone Deployment ---
   666→# Only created when proxy_mode is "standalone" (dev).
   667→# QA/prod use sidecar mode (injected via Helm values).
   668→
   669→resource "kubernetes_deployment_v1" "cloud_sql_proxy" {
   670→  count = local.service.database.proxy_mode == "standalone" ? 1 : 0
   671→
   672→  metadata {
   673→    name      = "cloud-sql-proxy"
   674→    namespace = local.service.namespaces.platform
   675→    labels = {
   676→      app        = "cloud-sql-proxy"
   677→      component  = "cloud-sql-proxy"
   678→      managed-by = "terraform"
   679→    }
   680→  }
   681→
   682→  spec {
   683→    replicas = 1
   684→
   685→    selector {
   686→      match_labels = {
   687→        app       = "cloud-sql-proxy"
   688→        component = "cloud-sql-proxy"
   689→      }
   690→    }
   691→
   692→    template {
   693→      metadata {
   694→        labels = {
   695→          app       = "cloud-sql-proxy"
   696→          component = "cloud-sql-proxy"
   697→        }
   698→      }
   699→
   700→      spec {
   701→        service_account_name = local.service.k8s_service_account
   702→
   703→        container {
   704→          name  = "cloud-sql-proxy"
   705→          image = local.global.cloud_sql.proxy_image
   706→
   707→          args = [
   708→            "--structured-logs",
   709→            "--private-ip",
   710→            "--auto-iam-authn",
   711→            "--address=0.0.0.0",
   712→            "--port=5432",
   713→            "--health-check",
   714→            "--http-port=9090",
   715→            "--http-address=0.0.0.0",
   716→            local.global.cloud_sql.connection_string,
   717→          ]
   718→
   719→          port {
   720→            container_port = 5432
   721→            name           = "postgres"
   722→          }
   723→
   724→          port {
   725→            container_port = 9090
   726→            name           = "http"
   727→          }
   728→
   729→          resources {
   730→            requests = {
   731→              cpu    = "100m"
   732→              memory = "128Mi"
   733→            }
   734→            limits = {
   735→              cpu    = "500m"
   736→              memory = "512Mi"
   737→            }
   738→          }
   739→
   740→          security_context {
   741→            run_as_non_root            = true
   742→            run_as_user                = 65532
   743→            allow_privilege_escalation = false
   744→          }
   745→
   746→          liveness_probe {
   747→            http_get {
   748→              path = "/liveness"
   749→              port = 9090
   750→            }
   751→            initial_delay_seconds = 30
   752→            period_seconds        = 10
   753→          }
   754→
   755→          readiness_probe {
   756→            http_get {
   757→              path = "/readiness"
   758→              port = 9090
   759→            }
   760→            initial_delay_seconds = 10
   761→            period_seconds        = 5
   762→          }
   763→        }
   764→      }
   765→    }
   766→  }
   767→
   768→  lifecycle {
   769→    ignore_changes = [
   770→      metadata[0].annotations,
   771→      spec[0].template[0].spec[0].container[0].resources,
   772→      spec[0].template[0].spec[0].container[0].security_context,
   773→      spec[0].template[0].spec[0].security_context,
   774→      spec[0].template[0].spec[0].toleration,
   775→    ]
   776→  }
   777→
   778→  depends_on = [
   779→    kubernetes_namespace_v1.crewai_platform,
   780→    kubernetes_service_account_v1.crewai_platform,
   781→  ]
   782→}
   783→
   784→resource "kubernetes_service_v1" "cloud_sql_proxy" {
   785→  count = local.service.database.proxy_mode == "standalone" ? 1 : 0
   786→
   787→  metadata {
   788→    name      = "cloud-sql-proxy"
   789→    namespace = local.service.namespaces.platform
   790→    labels = {
   791→      app        = "cloud-sql-proxy"
   792→      component  = "cloud-sql-proxy"
   793→      managed-by = "terraform"
   794→    }
   795→  }
   796→
   797→  spec {
   798→    selector = {
   799→      app       = "cloud-sql-proxy"
   800→      component = "cloud-sql-proxy"
   801→    }
   802→
   803→    port {
   804→      port        = 5432
   805→      target_port = 5432
   806→      name        = "postgres"
   807→    }
   808→
   809→    type = "ClusterIP"
   810→  }
   811→
   812→  depends_on = [kubernetes_namespace_v1.crewai_platform]
   813→}
   814→
   815→# --- Database Initialization Job ---
   816→# Grants IAM database user the privileges needed for CrewAI.
   817→# Re-trigger by changing var.database_init_trigger (e.g. v1 -> v2).
   818→
   819→resource "kubernetes_job_v1" "database_init" {
   820→  count = var.run_database_init_job ? 1 : 0
   821→
   822→  metadata {
   823→    name      = "crewai-db-init-${var.database_init_trigger}"
   824→    namespace = local.service.namespaces.platform
   825→    labels = {
   826→      app         = "crewai"
   827→      component   = "database-init"
   828→      environment = local.global.environment
   829→      managed-by  = "terraform"
   830→    }
   831→  }
   832→
   833→  spec {
   834→    ttl_seconds_after_finished = 300
   835→    backoff_limit              = 3
   836→
   837→    template {
   838→      metadata {
   839→        labels = {
   840→          app       = "crewai"
   841→          component = "database-init"
   842→        }
   843→      }
   844→
   845→      spec {
   846→        restart_policy       = "Never"
   847→        service_account_name = local.service.k8s_service_account
   848→
   849→        security_context {
   850→          run_as_non_root = true
   851→          run_as_user     = 1000
   852→          seccomp_profile {
   853→            type = "RuntimeDefault"
   854→          }
   855→        }
   856→
   857→        container {
   858→          name  = "psql"
   859→          image = "postgres:15-alpine"
   860→
   861→          security_context {
   862→            allow_privilege_escalation = false
   863→            privileged                 = false
   864→            run_as_non_root            = true
   865→            capabilities {
   866→              drop = ["ALL"]
   867→            }
   868→            read_only_root_filesystem = true
   869→          }
   870→
   871→          env {
   872→            name  = "PGHOST"
   873→            value = "127.0.0.1"
   874→          }
   875→          env {
   876→            name  = "PGPORT"
   877→            value = "5432"
   878→          }
   879→          env {
   880→            name  = "PGDATABASE"
   881→            value = local.service.database.names.production
   882→          }
   883→          env {
   884→            name  = "PGUSER"
   885→            value = "postgres"
   886→          }
   887→
   888→          command = ["/bin/sh", "-c"]
   889→          args = [
   890→            <<-EOT
   891→            set -e
   892→            echo "Waiting for Cloud SQL Proxy to be ready..."
   893→            for i in $(seq 1 30); do
   894→              if pg_isready -h 127.0.0.1 -p 5432 -U postgres > /dev/null 2>&1; then
   895→                echo "Cloud SQL Proxy is ready."
   896→                break
   897→              fi
   898→              if [ "$i" = "30" ]; then
   899→                echo "ERROR: Cloud SQL Proxy not ready after 60s"
   900→                exit 1
   901→              fi
   902→              echo "Attempt $i/30: proxy not ready, waiting..."
   903→              sleep 2
   904→            done
   905→
   906→            IAM_USER="${trimsuffix(google_service_account.crewai_platform.email, ".gserviceaccount.com")}"
   907→            echo "Granting privileges to IAM user: $IAM_USER"
   908→
   909→            for DB in ${local.service.database.names.production} ${local.service.database.names.cable} ${local.service.database.names.oauth}; do
   910→              echo "Granting privileges on database: $DB"
   911→              psql -d "$DB" -c "GRANT ALL PRIVILEGES ON SCHEMA public TO \"$IAM_USER\";"
   912→              psql -d "$DB" -c "GRANT ALL PRIVILEGES ON ALL TABLES IN SCHEMA public TO \"$IAM_USER\";"
   913→              psql -d "$DB" -c "GRANT ALL PRIVILEGES ON ALL SEQUENCES IN SCHEMA public TO \"$IAM_USER\";"
   914→              psql -d "$DB" -c "ALTER DEFAULT PRIVILEGES IN SCHEMA public GRANT ALL PRIVILEGES ON TABLES TO \"$IAM_USER\";"
   915→              psql -d "$DB" -c "ALTER DEFAULT PRIVILEGES IN SCHEMA public GRANT ALL PRIVILEGES ON SEQUENCES TO \"$IAM_USER\";"
   916→            done
   917→
   918→            echo "Database initialization complete!"
   919→
   920→            # Gracefully stop cloud-sql-proxy sidecar
   921→            echo "Stopping Cloud SQL Proxy sidecar..."
   922→            wget -q -O- http://localhost:9091/quitquitquit || true
   923→            EOT
   924→          ]
   925→
   926→          resources {
   927→            requests = {
   928→              cpu    = "100m"
   929→              memory = "128Mi"
   930→            }
   931→            limits = {
   932→              memory = "256Mi"
   933→            }
   934→          }
   935→        }
   936→
   937→        container {
   938→          name  = "cloud-sql-proxy"
   939→          image = local.global.cloud_sql.proxy_image
   940→          args = [
   941→            "--private-ip",
   942→            "--auto-iam-authn",
   943→            "--quitquitquit",
   944→            "--exit-zero-on-sigterm",
   945→            local.global.cloud_sql.connection_string
   946→          ]
   947→
   948→          security_context {
   949→            allow_privilege_escalation = false
   950→            capabilities {
   951→              drop = ["ALL"]
   952→            }
   953→            run_as_non_root = true
   954→          }
   955→
   956→          resources {
   957→            requests = {
   958→              cpu    = "100m"
   959→              memory = "128Mi"
   960→            }
   961→            limits = {
   962→              memory = "256Mi"
   963→            }
   964→          }
   965→        }
   966→      }
   967→    }
   968→  }
   969→
   970→  wait_for_completion = true
   971→
   972→  timeouts {
   973→    create = "5m"
   974→    update = "5m"
   975→  }
   976→
   977→  depends_on = [
```

> AGENT

I can see the setup. For **dev**, you have three Cloud SQL databases on `kd-ix-eur-dev-cloudsql-platform`:

| Database | Purpose |
|----------|---------|
| `crewai_plus_production` | Main Rails database |
| `crewai_plus_cable_production` | ActionCable |
| `crewai_plus_oauth_db` | OAuth |

The Cloud SQL Proxy is already running as a standalone deployment in the `crewai-platform` namespace, so the cleanest way to purge all tables is to connect through it.

> TOOL

> AGENT

Dev only. Here's the approach to purge all tables in the three databases. Since the Cloud SQL Proxy is running standalone in `crewai-platform`, you can spin up a temporary psql pod to connect through it.

**Run a one-shot psql pod connected to the Cloud SQL Proxy:**

```bash
kubectl run psql-cleanup --rm -it \
  --namespace=crewai-platform \
  --image=postgres:15-alpine \
  --restart=Never \
  --env="PGHOST=cloud-sql-proxy" \
  --env="PGPORT=5432" \
  --env="PGUSER=postgres" \
  -- /bin/sh
```

Then inside the pod, run this against each database:

```sql
-- 1. Production database
psql -d crewai_plus_production -c "DROP SCHEMA public CASCADE; CREATE SCHEMA public; GRANT ALL ON SCHEMA public TO \"crewai-platform@kd-ix-eur-dev-gke.iam\";"

-- 2. Cable database
psql -d crewai_plus_cable_production -c "DROP SCHEMA public CASCADE; CREATE SCHEMA public; GRANT ALL ON SCHEMA public TO \"crewai-platform@kd-ix-eur-dev-gke.iam\";"

-- 3. OAuth database
psql -d crewai_plus_oauth_db -c "DROP SCHEMA public CASCADE; CREATE SCHEMA public; GRANT ALL ON SCHEMA public TO \"crewai-platform@kd-ix-eur-dev-gke.iam\";"
```

The `DROP SCHEMA public CASCADE` drops all tables, views, functions, etc. in one shot. The `CREATE SCHEMA public` recreates the empty schema, and the `GRANT` re-establishes access for the IAM service account so Rails can run migrations on next startup.

After exiting the pod, restart the CrewAI deployments to trigger Rails migrations:

```bash
kubectl rollout restart deployment -n crewai-platform
```

Rails will detect empty databases and run all migrations to rebuild the schema with the new encryption key.

**Key note:** The Cloud SQL Proxy in dev uses IAM auth (`--auto-iam-authn`), but connecting as `postgres` (the built-in superuser) should work for the cleanup since the proxy also accepts password-based auth for the postgres user. If the postgres password isn't available, you may need to reset it via `gcloud sql users set-password postgres --instance=kd-ix-eur-dev-cloudsql-platform --project=kd-ix-eur-dev-platform-data --password=<new-password>` first.

> DEVELOPER

psql: error: connection to server at "cloud-sql-proxy" (10.28.2.168), port 5432 failed: fe_sendauth: no password supplied

> AGENT

The proxy requires a password for the `postgres` user. You'll need to set one via `gcloud` first:

```bash
gcloud sql users set-password postgres \
  --instance=kd-ix-eur-dev-cloudsql-platform \
  --project=kd-ix-eur-dev-platform-data \
  --password='TempCleanup2026!'
```

Then back in the psql pod, set the password and run the commands:

```bash
export PGPASSWORD='TempCleanup2026!'

psql -d crewai_plus_production -c "DROP SCHEMA public CASCADE; CREATE SCHEMA public; GRANT ALL ON SCHEMA public TO \"crewai-platform@kd-ix-eur-dev-gke.iam\";"

psql -d crewai_plus_cable_production -c "DROP SCHEMA public CASCADE; CREATE SCHEMA public; GRANT ALL ON SCHEMA public TO \"crewai-platform@kd-ix-eur-dev-gke.iam\";"

psql -d crewai_plus_oauth_db -c "DROP SCHEMA public CASCADE; CREATE SCHEMA public; GRANT ALL ON SCHEMA public TO \"crewai-platform@kd-ix-eur-dev-gke.iam\";"
```

After you're done, consider resetting the postgres password to something random or disabling it if it wasn't set before, so you don't leave a known credential on the instance.

> DEVELOPER

ok, lets clear up

> AGENT

Reset the postgres password to something random so the temporary credential isn't left behind:

```bash
gcloud sql users set-password postgres \
  --instance=kd-ix-eur-dev-cloudsql-platform \
  --project=kd-ix-eur-dev-platform-data \
  --password="$(openssl rand -base64 32)"
```

Then restart the CrewAI deployments so Rails runs migrations and rebuilds the schema with the new encryption key:

```bash
kubectl rollout restart deployment -n crewai-platform
```

You can watch the rollout with:

```bash
kubectl get pods -n crewai-platform -w
```

Check the web pod logs for migration output to confirm tables are being recreated:

```bash
kubectl logs -n crewai-platform -l app.kubernetes.io/component=web -f
```

> DEVELOPER

there was no password before, how do i remove it

> AGENT

For Cloud SQL, you can set it to an empty string to effectively remove it:

```bash
gcloud sql users set-password postgres \
  --instance=kd-ix-eur-dev-cloudsql-platform \
  --project=kd-ix-eur-dev-platform-data \
  --password=''
```

This returns it to the previous state where the `postgres` user has no password set, and only IAM-authenticated users can connect through the proxy.

> DEVELOPER

ERROR: (gcloud.sql.users.set-password) HTTPError 400: Invalid request: Missing user password for PostgreSQL instance.

> AGENT

GCloud won't accept an empty password. Since the instance uses IAM auth exclusively (no password-based access was needed before), the safest approach is to just leave a strong random password on it:

```bash
gcloud sql users set-password postgres \
  --instance=kd-ix-eur-dev-cloudsql-platform \
  --project=kd-ix-eur-dev-platform-data \
  --password="$(openssl rand -base64 32)"
```

This is effectively the same as "no password" from an operational standpoint - nobody knows it, and all your workloads authenticate via IAM through the Cloud SQL Proxy (`--auto-iam-authn`). The postgres user password is only needed for ad-hoc admin access like what we just did.

> DEVELOPER

nope, definetly need to clear the password

> AGENT

The `gcloud` CLI won't accept an empty password, but you can hit the Cloud SQL Admin API directly to clear it:

```bash
curl -X PUT \
  "https://sqladmin.googleapis.com/v1/projects/kd-ix-eur-dev-platform-data/instances/kd-ix-eur-dev-cloudsql-platform/users?name=postgres&host=" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer $(gcloud auth print-access-token)" \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"password": ""}'
```

```
     1→#!/usr/bin/env bash
     2→# Generate environment-specific Helm values from config.json
     3→# Usage: ./scripts/generate-helm-values.sh <environment> [--check]
     4→#
     5→# Generates TWO files per environment:
     6→#   1. environments/{env}/helm/values-upstream.yaml    - OCI chart overrides
     7→#   2. environments/{env}/helm/crewai-gateway/values-{env}.yaml - Gateway sidecar chart
     8→#
     9→# Arguments:
    10→#   environment  - Target environment (dev, qa, prod)
    11→#   --check      - Compare generated output against existing files; exit 1 if different
    12→
    13→set -euo pipefail
    14→
    15→SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
    16→REPO_ROOT="$(cd "$SCRIPT_DIR/.." && pwd)"
    17→
    18→# --- Argument parsing ---
    19→if [[ $# -lt 1 ]]; then
    20→  echo "Usage: $0 <environment> [--check]" >&2
    21→  exit 1
    22→fi
    23→
    24→ENV="$1"
    25→CHECK_MODE=false
    26→if [[ "${2:-}" == "--check" ]]; then
    27→  CHECK_MODE=true
    28→fi
    29→
    30→CONFIG_FILE="$REPO_ROOT/environments/$ENV/config.json"
    31→UPSTREAM_FILE="$REPO_ROOT/environments/$ENV/helm/values-upstream.yaml"
    32→GATEWAY_FILE="$REPO_ROOT/environments/$ENV/helm/crewai-gateway/values-${ENV}.yaml"
    33→
    34→if [[ ! -f "$CONFIG_FILE" ]]; then
    35→  echo "Error: Config file not found: $CONFIG_FILE" >&2
    36→  exit 1
    37→fi
    38→
    39→# --- Verify dependencies ---
    40→for cmd in jq; do
    41→  if ! command -v "$cmd" &>/dev/null; then
    42→    echo "Error: Required command '$cmd' not found" >&2
    43→    exit 1
    44→  fi
    45→done
    46→
    47→# --- Generate upstream values (OCI chart overrides) ---
    48→generate_upstream() {
    49→  local config="$1"
    50→  local env="$2"
    51→
    52→  # Extract values from config
    53→  local environment hostname image_registry image_prefix crew_registry
    54→  local sa_name gcp_sa_email
    55→  local web_replicas worker_replicas web_port web_use_https
    56→  local web_req_cpu web_req_mem web_lim_cpu web_lim_mem
    57→  local worker_req_cpu worker_req_mem worker_lim_cpu worker_lim_mem
    58→  local buildkit_enabled buildkit_rootless
    59→  local bk_req_cpu bk_req_mem bk_lim_cpu bk_lim_mem
    60→  local auth_provider db_host db_port db_user
    61→  local db_production db_cable db_oauth
    62→  local aws_bucket storage_service
    63→  local eso_refresh eso_secret_name eso_gcp_project
    64→  local eso_cluster_location eso_cluster_name eso_cluster_project
    65→  local gcs_secret_name rails_secret_name
    66→  local proxy_mode replicated_airgap replicated_repo
    67→  local platform_ns crews_ns
    68→
    69→  environment=$(jq -r '.global.environment' "$config")
    70→  hostname=$(jq -r '.service.hostname' "$config")
    71→  image_registry=$(jq -r '.service.image.registry' "$config")
    72→  image_prefix=$(jq -r '.service.image.prefix_override' "$config")
    73→  crew_registry=$(jq -r '.service.image.crew_registry' "$config")
    74→  sa_name=$(jq -r '.service.k8s_service_account' "$config")
    75→  gcp_sa_email=$(jq -r '.service.gcp_service_account.email' "$config")
    76→  platform_ns=$(jq -r '.service.namespaces.platform' "$config")
    77→  crews_ns=$(jq -r '.service.namespaces.crews' "$config")
    78→
    79→  web_replicas=$(jq -r '.service.web.replicas' "$config")
    80→  worker_replicas=$(jq -r '.service.worker.replicas' "$config")
    81→  web_port=$(jq -r '.service.web.port' "$config")
    82→  web_use_https=$(jq -r '.service.web.use_https' "$config")
    83→  web_req_cpu=$(jq -r '.service.web.resources.requests.cpu' "$config")
    84→  web_req_mem=$(jq -r '.service.web.resources.requests.memory' "$config")
    85→  web_lim_cpu=$(jq -r '.service.web.resources.limits.cpu' "$config")
    86→  web_lim_mem=$(jq -r '.service.web.resources.limits.memory' "$config")
    87→
    88→  worker_req_cpu=$(jq -r '.service.worker.resources.requests.cpu' "$config")
    89→  worker_req_mem=$(jq -r '.service.worker.resources.requests.memory' "$config")
    90→  worker_lim_cpu=$(jq -r '.service.worker.resources.limits.cpu' "$config")
    91→  worker_lim_mem=$(jq -r '.service.worker.resources.limits.memory' "$config")
    92→
    93→  buildkit_enabled=$(jq -r '.service.buildkit.enabled' "$config")
    94→  buildkit_rootless=$(jq -r '.service.buildkit.rootless' "$config")
    95→  bk_req_cpu=$(jq -r '.service.buildkit.resources.requests.cpu' "$config")
    96→  bk_req_mem=$(jq -r '.service.buildkit.resources.requests.memory' "$config")
    97→  bk_lim_cpu=$(jq -r '.service.buildkit.resources.limits.cpu' "$config")
    98→  bk_lim_mem=$(jq -r '.service.buildkit.resources.limits.memory' "$config")
    99→
   100→  auth_provider=$(jq -r '.service.auth.provider' "$config")
   101→  proxy_mode=$(jq -r '.service.database.proxy_mode' "$config")
   102→  db_user=$(jq -r '.service.database.iam_user' "$config")
   103→  db_production=$(jq -r '.service.database.names.production' "$config")
   104→  db_cable=$(jq -r '.service.database.names.cable' "$config")
   105→  db_oauth=$(jq -r '.service.database.names.oauth' "$config")
   106→
   107→  eso_refresh=$(jq -r '.service.external_secrets.refresh_interval' "$config")
   108→  eso_secret_name=$(jq -r '.service.secrets.target_secret_name' "$config")
   109→  eso_gcp_project=$(jq -r '.global.project_ids.security' "$config")
   110→  eso_cluster_location=$(jq -r '.service.external_secrets.cluster_location' "$config")
   111→  eso_cluster_name=$(jq -r '.service.external_secrets.cluster_name' "$config")
   112→  eso_cluster_project=$(jq -r '.service.external_secrets.cluster_project_id' "$config")
   113→  gcs_secret_name=$(jq -r '.service.secrets.gcs_credentials' "$config")
   114→  rails_secret_name=$(jq -r '.service.secrets.rails_secrets' "$config")
   115→
   116→  replicated_airgap=$(jq -r '.service.replicated.is_airgap' "$config")
   117→  replicated_repo=$(jq -r '.service.replicated.image_repository' "$config")
   118→
   119→  local cloudsql_connection
   120→  cloudsql_connection=$(jq -r '.global.cloud_sql.connection_string' "$config")
   121→
   122→  # Determine DB host based on proxy mode
   123→  if [[ "$proxy_mode" == "standalone" ]]; then
   124→    db_host="cloud-sql-proxy"
   125→    db_port="5432"
   126→  else
   127→    db_host="127.0.0.1"
   128→    db_port="5432"
   129→  fi
   130→
   131→  # Cloud SQL sidecar block (only for sidecar mode)
   132→  local SIDECAR_BLOCK=""
   133→  if [[ "$proxy_mode" == "sidecar" ]]; then
   134→    local proxy_image
   135→    proxy_image=$(jq -r '.global.cloud_sql.proxy_image' "$config")
   136→    local proxy_req_cpu proxy_req_mem proxy_lim_cpu proxy_lim_mem
   137→    proxy_req_cpu=$(jq -r '.service.web.cloud_sql_proxy.resources.requests.cpu // "100m"' "$config")
   138→    proxy_req_mem=$(jq -r '.service.web.cloud_sql_proxy.resources.requests.memory // "128Mi"' "$config")
   139→    proxy_lim_cpu=$(jq -r '.service.web.cloud_sql_proxy.resources.limits.cpu // "500m"' "$config")
   140→    proxy_lim_mem=$(jq -r '.service.web.cloud_sql_proxy.resources.limits.memory // "512Mi"' "$config")
   141→
   142→    SIDECAR_BLOCK="
   143→# Cloud SQL Proxy sidecar (injected into web/worker pods)
   144→web:
   145→  extraContainers:
   146→    - name: cloud-sql-proxy
   147→      image: \"${proxy_image}\"
   148→      args:
   149→        - \"--structured-logs\"
   150→        - \"--private-ip\"
   151→        - \"--auto-iam-authn\"
   152→        - \"--port=5432\"
   153→        - \"--health-check\"
   154→        - \"--http-port=9090\"
   155→        - \"--http-address=0.0.0.0\"
   156→        - \"${cloudsql_connection}\"
   157→      ports:
   158→        - containerPort: 9090
   159→          name: health
   160→      livenessProbe:
   161→        httpGet:
   162→          path: /liveness
   163→          port: 9090
   164→        initialDelaySeconds: 30
   165→        periodSeconds: 10
   166→      readinessProbe:
   167→        httpGet:
   168→          path: /readiness
   169→          port: 9090
   170→        initialDelaySeconds: 10
   171→        periodSeconds: 5
   172→      resources:
   173→        requests:
   174→          cpu: \"${proxy_req_cpu}\"
   175→          memory: \"${proxy_req_mem}\"
   176→        limits:
   177→          cpu: \"${proxy_lim_cpu}\"
   178→          memory: \"${proxy_lim_mem}\"
   179→      securityContext:
   180→        runAsNonRoot: true
   181→        runAsUser: 65532
   182→        allowPrivilegeEscalation: false
   183→        readOnlyRootFilesystem: true
   184→        capabilities:
   185→          drop:
   186→            - ALL"
   187→  fi
   188→
   189→  # WorkOS env vars block (only for dev where credentials are in config)
   190→  local WORKOS_BLOCK=""
   191→  local workos_client_id workos_authkit_domain workos_api_key workos_cookie_password
   192→  workos_client_id=$(jq -r '.service.auth.workos.client_id // empty' "$config")
   193→  if [[ -n "$workos_client_id" ]]; then
   194→    workos_authkit_domain=$(jq -r '.service.auth.workos.authkit_domain' "$config")
   195→    workos_api_key=$(jq -r '.service.auth.workos.api_key' "$config")
   196→    workos_cookie_password=$(jq -r '.service.auth.workos.cookie_password' "$config")
   197→    WORKOS_BLOCK="  WORKOS_CLIENT_ID: \"${workos_client_id}\"
   198→  WORKOS_AUTHKIT_DOMAIN: \"${workos_authkit_domain}\"
   199→  WORKOS_API_KEY: \"${workos_api_key}\"
   200→  WORKOS_COOKIE_PASSWORD: \"${workos_cookie_password}\""
   201→  fi
   202→
   203→  # HPA block (only for prod)
   204→  local HPA_BLOCK=""
   205→  local hpa_enabled
   206→  hpa_enabled=$(jq -r '.service.hpa.enabled' "$config")
   207→  if [[ "$hpa_enabled" == "true" ]]; then
   208→    local hpa_min hpa_max hpa_cpu hpa_mem
   209→    hpa_min=$(jq -r '.service.hpa.min_replicas // 3' "$config")
   210→    hpa_max=$(jq -r '.service.hpa.max_replicas // 10' "$config")
   211→    hpa_cpu=$(jq -r '.service.hpa.target_cpu // 70' "$config")
   212→    hpa_mem=$(jq -r '.service.hpa.target_memory // 80' "$config")
   213→    HPA_BLOCK="
   214→# Horizontal Pod Autoscaler
   215→hpa:
   216→  enabled: true
   217→  minReplicas: ${hpa_min}
   218→  maxReplicas: ${hpa_max}
   219→  targetCPUUtilizationPercentage: ${hpa_cpu}
   220→  targetMemoryUtilizationPercentage: ${hpa_mem}"
   221→  fi
   222→
   223→  cat <<EOF
   224→# AUTO-GENERATED from config.json - DO NOT EDIT MANUALLY
   225→# Regenerate with: ./scripts/generate-helm-values.sh ${env}
   226→
   227→# =============================================================================
   228→# GLOBAL SETTINGS
   229→# =============================================================================
   230→
   231→global:
   232→  fullnameOverride: "crewai-platform"
   233→  imageRegistry: "${image_registry}"
   234→  imageNamePrefixOverride: "${image_prefix}"
   235→
   236→replicated:
   237→  isAirgap: ${replicated_airgap}
   238→  image:
   239→    repository: "${replicated_repo}"
   240→
   241→crewNamespace: "${crews_ns}"
   242→serviceAccount: "${sa_name}"
   243→
   244→# =============================================================================
   245→# WEB COMPONENT
   246→# =============================================================================
   247→
   248→web:
   249→  name: "web"
   250→  replicaCount: ${web_replicas}
   251→  useHttps: ${web_use_https}
   252→  enableSslFromPuma: false
   253→  port: ${web_port}
   254→  tls:
   255→    autoGenerate: false
   256→  resources:
   257→    requests:
   258→      cpu: "${web_req_cpu}"
   259→      memory: "${web_req_mem}"
   260→    limits:
   261→      cpu: "${web_lim_cpu}"
   262→      memory: "${web_lim_mem}"
   263→  livenessProbe:
   264→    httpGet:
   265→      path: /health
   266→      port: ${web_port}
   267→      scheme: HTTP
   268→    initialDelaySeconds: 60
   269→    periodSeconds: 10
   270→    timeoutSeconds: 5
   271→    failureThreshold: 3
   272→  readinessProbe:
   273→    httpGet:
   274→      path: /health
   275→      port: ${web_port}
   276→      scheme: HTTP
   277→    initialDelaySeconds: 30
   278→    periodSeconds: 5
   279→    timeoutSeconds: 3
   280→    failureThreshold: 3
   281→  startupProbe:
   282→    httpGet:
   283→      path: /health
   284→      port: ${web_port}
   285→      scheme: HTTP
   286→    initialDelaySeconds: 0
   287→    periodSeconds: 10
   288→    timeoutSeconds: 5
   289→    failureThreshold: 30
   290→  podSecurityContext:
   291→    runAsNonRoot: true
   292→    runAsUser: 1000
   293→    runAsGroup: 1000
   294→    fsGroup: 1000
   295→    fsGroupChangePolicy: "OnRootMismatch"
   296→    seccompProfile:
   297→      type: RuntimeDefault
   298→  securityContext:
   299→    runAsNonRoot: true
   300→    runAsUser: 1000
   301→    allowPrivilegeEscalation: false
   302→    capabilities:
   303→      drop:
   304→        - ALL
   305→    seccompProfile:
   306→      type: RuntimeDefault
   307→  terminationGracePeriodSeconds: 30
   308→  service:
   309→    type: ClusterIP
   310→    annotations:
   311→      cloud.google.com/neg: '{"ingress":true,"exposed_ports":{"${web_port}":{}}}'
   312→  ingress:
   313→    enabled: false
   314→
   315→# =============================================================================
   316→# WORKER COMPONENT
   317→# =============================================================================
   318→
   319→worker:
   320→  replicaCount: ${worker_replicas}
   321→  resources:
   322→    requests:
   323→      cpu: "${worker_req_cpu}"
   324→      memory: "${worker_req_mem}"
   325→    limits:
   326→      cpu: "${worker_lim_cpu}"
   327→      memory: "${worker_lim_mem}"
   328→  livenessProbe:
   329→    exec:
   330→      command:
   331→        - /bin/sh
   332→        - -c
   333→        - pgrep -f worker
   334→    initialDelaySeconds: 60
   335→    periodSeconds: 30
   336→    timeoutSeconds: 5
   337→    failureThreshold: 3
   338→  readinessProbe:
   339→    exec:
   340→      command:
   341→        - /bin/sh
   342→        - -c
   343→        - pgrep -f worker
   344→    initialDelaySeconds: 30
   345→    periodSeconds: 10
   346→    timeoutSeconds: 3
   347→    failureThreshold: 3
   348→  podSecurityContext:
   349→    runAsNonRoot: true
   350→    runAsUser: 1000
   351→    runAsGroup: 1000
   352→    fsGroup: 1000
   353→    fsGroupChangePolicy: "OnRootMismatch"
   354→    seccompProfile:
   355→      type: RuntimeDefault
   356→  securityContext:
   357→    runAsNonRoot: true
   358→    runAsUser: 1000
   359→    allowPrivilegeEscalation: false
   360→    capabilities:
   361→      drop:
   362→        - ALL
   363→    seccompProfile:
   364→      type: RuntimeDefault
   365→
   366→# =============================================================================
   367→# BUILDKIT COMPONENT
   368→# =============================================================================
   369→
   370→buildkit:
   371→  enabled: ${buildkit_enabled}
   372→  replicaCount: 1
   373→  rootless:
   374→    enabled: ${buildkit_rootless}
   375→    runAsUser: 1000
   376→    runAsGroup: 1000
   377→    fsGroup: 1000
   378→  resources:
   379→    requests:
   380→      cpu: "${bk_req_cpu}"
   381→      memory: "${bk_req_mem}"
   382→    limits:
   383→      cpu: "${bk_lim_cpu}"
   384→      memory: "${bk_lim_mem}"
   385→  podSecurityContext:
   386→    runAsNonRoot: true
   387→    runAsUser: 1000
   388→    runAsGroup: 1000
   389→    fsGroup: 1000
   390→    fsGroupChangePolicy: "OnRootMismatch"
   391→    seccompProfile:
   392→      type: Unconfined
   393→  securityContext:
   394→    runAsNonRoot: true
   395→    runAsUser: 1000
   396→    runAsGroup: 1000
   397→    allowPrivilegeEscalation: false
   398→    capabilities:
   399→      drop:
   400→        - ALL
   401→      add:
   402→        - CHOWN
   403→        - DAC_OVERRIDE
   404→        - FOWNER
   405→        - SETGID
   406→        - SETUID
   407→    seccompProfile:
   408→      type: Unconfined
   409→    appArmorProfile:
   410→      type: Unconfined
   411→
   412→# =============================================================================
   413→# DISABLED INTERNAL SERVICES
   414→# =============================================================================
   415→
   416→postgres:
   417→  enabled: false
   418→
   419→minio:
   420→  enabled: false
   421→
   422→oauth:
   423→  enabled: false
   424→
   425→# =============================================================================
   426→# ENVIRONMENT VARIABLES
   427→# =============================================================================
   428→
   429→envVars:
   430→  APPLICATION_HOST: "${hostname}"
   431→  RAILS_ENV: "production"
   432→  CREW_IMAGE_REGISTRY_OVERRIDE: "${crew_registry}"
   433→  DB_HOST: "${db_host}"
   434→  DB_PORT: "${db_port}"
   435→  DB_USER: "${db_user}"
   436→  POSTGRES_DB: "${db_production}"
   437→  POSTGRES_CABLE_DB: "${db_cable}"
   438→  POSTGRES_OAUTH_DB: "${db_oauth}"
   439→  STORAGE_SERVICE: "amazon"
   440→  AWS_REGION: "auto"
   441→  AWS_BUCKET: "crewai-data-${environment}"
   442→  AWS_ENDPOINT: "https://storage.googleapis.com"
   443→  AUTH_PROVIDER: "${auth_provider}"
   444→  WORKOS_REDIRECT_URI: "https://${hostname}/auth/workos/callback"
   445→$(if [[ -n "$WORKOS_BLOCK" ]]; then echo "$WORKOS_BLOCK"; fi)
   446→
   447→envFrom:
   448→  - secretRef:
   449→      name: "${eso_secret_name}"
   450→
   451→# =============================================================================
   452→# EXTERNAL SECRETS OPERATOR
   453→# =============================================================================
   454→
   455→externalSecrets:
   456→  enabled: true
   457→  refreshInterval: "${eso_refresh}"
   458→  targetSecretName: "${eso_secret_name}"
   459→  gcpProjectId: "${eso_gcp_project}"
   460→  auth:
   461→    workloadIdentity:
   462→      enabled: true
   463→      clusterLocation: "${eso_cluster_location}"
   464→      clusterName: "${eso_cluster_name}"
   465→      clusterProjectID: "${eso_cluster_project}"
   466→      serviceAccountName: "${sa_name}"
   467→  secrets:
   468→    railsSecrets:
   469→      secretName: "${rails_secret_name}"
   470→    gcsCredentials:
   471→      secretName: "${gcs_secret_name}"
   472→${SIDECAR_BLOCK}
   473→${HPA_BLOCK}
   474→EOF
   475→}
   476→
   477→# --- Generate gateway values (sidecar chart) ---
   478→generate_gateway() {
   479→  local config="$1"
   480→  local env="$2"
   481→
   482→  local hostname cert_map gateway_enabled gateway_class
   483→  local timeout_sec drain_sec
   484→  local np_enabled pdb_enabled ca_enabled
   485→  local platform_ns
   486→  local db_iam_user db_production db_cable db_oauth replicated_airgap
   487→
   488→  hostname=$(jq -r '.service.hostname' "$config")
   489→  cert_map=$(jq -r '.service.certificate.map' "$config")
   490→  gateway_enabled=$(jq -r '.service.gateway.enabled' "$config")
   491→  gateway_class=$(jq -r '.service.gateway.class_name' "$config")
   492→  timeout_sec=$(jq -r '.service.gateway.timeout_sec // 300' "$config")
   493→  drain_sec=$(jq -r '.service.gateway.connection_draining_sec // 60' "$config")
   494→  np_enabled=$(jq -r '.service.network_policies.enabled' "$config")
   495→  pdb_enabled=$(jq -r '.service.pdb.enabled' "$config")
   496→  ca_enabled=$(jq -r '.service.cloud_armor.enabled' "$config")
   497→  platform_ns=$(jq -r '.service.namespaces.platform' "$config")
   498→  db_iam_user=$(jq -r '.service.database.iam_user' "$config")
   499→  db_production=$(jq -r '.service.database.names.production' "$config")
   500→  db_cable=$(jq -r '.service.database.names.cable' "$config")
   501→  db_oauth=$(jq -r '.service.database.names.oauth' "$config")
   502→  replicated_airgap=$(jq -r '.service.replicated.is_airgap' "$config")
   503→
   504→  local web_port service_name
   505→  web_port=$(jq -r '.service.web.port' "$config")
   506→  service_name="crewai-platform-web"
   507→
   508→  local eso_gcp_project eso_cluster_location eso_cluster_name eso_cluster_project
   509→  local sa_name gcs_secret_name rails_secret_name eso_secret_name
   510→  local workos_secret_name github_secret_name replicated_secret_name
   511→  eso_gcp_project=$(jq -r '.global.project_ids.security' "$config")
   512→  eso_cluster_location=$(jq -r '.service.external_secrets.cluster_location' "$config")
   513→  eso_cluster_name=$(jq -r '.service.external_secrets.cluster_name' "$config")
   514→  eso_cluster_project=$(jq -r '.service.external_secrets.cluster_project_id' "$config")
   515→  sa_name=$(jq -r '.service.k8s_service_account' "$config")
   516→  gcs_secret_name=$(jq -r '.service.secrets.gcs_credentials' "$config")
   517→  rails_secret_name=$(jq -r '.service.secrets.rails_secrets' "$config")
   518→  workos_secret_name=$(jq -r '.service.secrets.workos_credentials' "$config")
   519→  github_secret_name=$(jq -r '.service.secrets.github_credentials' "$config")
   520→  replicated_secret_name=$(jq -r '.service.secrets.replicated_credentials' "$config")
   521→  eso_secret_name=$(jq -r '.service.secrets.target_secret_name' "$config")
   522→
   523→  cat <<EOF
   524→# AUTO-GENERATED from config.json - DO NOT EDIT MANUALLY
   525→# Regenerate with: ./scripts/generate-helm-values.sh ${env}
   526→
   527→# Backend service reference
   528→crewai:
   529→  namespace: "${platform_ns}"
   530→  serviceName: "${service_name}"
   531→  servicePort: ${web_port}
   532→  serviceProtocol: "HTTP"
   533→
   534→# GKE Gateway
   535→gateway:
   536→  enabled: ${gateway_enabled}
   537→  name: "crewai-${env}-gateway"
   538→  gatewayClassName: "${gateway_class}"
   539→  certificateMap: "${cert_map}"
   540→  listeners:
   541→    http:
   542→      enabled: true
   543→      name: "http"
   544→      port: 80
   545→      allowedRoutes:
   546→        namespaces:
   547→          from: "Same"
   548→    https:
   549→      enabled: true
   550→      name: "https"
   551→      port: 443
   552→      hostname: "${hostname}"
   553→      allowedRoutes:
   554→        namespaces:
   555→          from: "Same"
   556→
   557→# HTTPRoute
   558→httpRoute:
   559→  enabled: true
   560→  hostnames:
   561→    - "${hostname}"
   562→  parentRefs:
   563→    - name: ""
   564→      sectionName: "https"
   565→  rules:
   566→    - matches:
   567→        - path:
   568→            type: PathPrefix
   569→            value: /
   570→      backendRefs:
   571→        - name: ""
   572→          port: ${web_port}
   573→
   574→# HTTP-to-HTTPS redirect
   575→httpRedirectRoute:
   576→  enabled: true
   577→  hostnames:
   578→    - "${hostname}"
   579→  parentRefs:
   580→    - name: ""
   581→      sectionName: "http"
   582→  rules:
   583→    - matches:
   584→        - path:
   585→            type: PathPrefix
   586→            value: /
   587→      filters:
   588→        - type: RequestRedirect
   589→          requestRedirect:
   590→            scheme: https
   591→            statusCode: 301
   592→
   593→# GCPBackendPolicy
   594→gcpBackendPolicy:
   595→  enabled: true
   596→  timeoutSec: ${timeout_sec}
   597→  connectionDraining:
   598→    drainingTimeoutSec: ${drain_sec}
   599→  logging:
   600→    enabled: true
   601→    sampleRate: 1.0
   602→
   603→# HealthCheckPolicy
   604→healthCheckPolicy:
   605→  enabled: true
   606→  default:
   607→    checkIntervalSec: 15
   608→    timeoutSec: 5
   609→    healthyThreshold: 2
   610→    unhealthyThreshold: 3
   611→    config:
   612→      type: "HTTP"
   613→      requestPath: "/health"
   614→      port: ${web_port}
   615→
   616→# Feature toggles
   617→oauth:
   618→  enabled: false
   619→
   620→networkPolicy:
   621→  enabled: ${np_enabled}
   622→
   623→podDisruptionBudget:
   624→  enabled: ${pdb_enabled}
   625→
   626→cloudArmor:
   627→  enabled: ${ca_enabled}
   628→
   629→# External Secrets Operator
   630→externalSecrets:
   631→  enabled: true
   632→  refreshInterval: "1h"
   633→  targetSecretName: "${eso_secret_name}"
   634→  gcpProjectId: "${eso_gcp_project}"
   635→  auth:
   636→    workloadIdentity:
   637→      enabled: true
   638→      clusterLocation: "${eso_cluster_location}"
   639→      clusterName: "${eso_cluster_name}"
   640→      clusterProjectID: "${eso_cluster_project}"
   641→      serviceAccountName: "${sa_name}"
   642→  secrets:
   643→    railsSecrets:
   644→      secretName: "${rails_secret_name}"
   645→    gcsCredentials:
   646→      secretName: "${gcs_secret_name}"
   647→    workosCredentials:
   648→      secretName: "${workos_secret_name}"
   649→    githubCredentials:
   650→      secretName: "${github_secret_name}"
   651→    replicatedCredentials:
   652→      secretName: "${replicated_secret_name}"
   653→
   654→# Database permissions job
   655→databasePermissions:
   656→  iamUser: "${db_iam_user}"
   657→  databases:
   658→    production: "${db_production}"
   659→    cable: "${db_cable}"
   660→    oauth: "${db_oauth}"
   661→
   662→# Replicated SDK
   663→replicated:
   664→  isAirgap: ${replicated_airgap}
   665→
   666→# Global
   667→global:
   668→  environment: "${env}"
   669→EOF
   670→}
   671→
   672→# --- Generate both files ---
   673→UPSTREAM_GENERATED=$(generate_upstream "$CONFIG_FILE" "$ENV")
   674→GATEWAY_GENERATED=$(generate_gateway "$CONFIG_FILE" "$ENV")
   675→
   676→if [[ "$CHECK_MODE" == true ]]; then
   677→  FAILED=false
   678→
   679→  if [[ ! -f "$UPSTREAM_FILE" ]]; then
   680→    echo "Error: Upstream file does not exist: $UPSTREAM_FILE" >&2
   681→    FAILED=true
   682→  elif ! diff <(echo "$UPSTREAM_GENERATED") "$UPSTREAM_FILE" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
   683→    echo "MISMATCH: $UPSTREAM_FILE does not match config.json" >&2
   684→    diff <(echo "$UPSTREAM_GENERATED") "$UPSTREAM_FILE" >&2 || true
   685→    FAILED=true
   686→  else
   687→    echo "OK: $UPSTREAM_FILE matches config.json"
   688→  fi
   689→
   690→  if [[ ! -f "$GATEWAY_FILE" ]]; then
   691→    echo "Error: Gateway file does not exist: $GATEWAY_FILE" >&2
   692→    FAILED=true
   693→  elif ! diff <(echo "$GATEWAY_GENERATED") "$GATEWAY_FILE" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
   694→    echo "MISMATCH: $GATEWAY_FILE does not match config.json" >&2
   695→    diff <(echo "$GATEWAY_GENERATED") "$GATEWAY_FILE" >&2 || true
   696→    FAILED=true
   697→  else
   698→    echo "OK: $GATEWAY_FILE matches config.json"
   699→  fi
   700→
   701→  if [[ "$FAILED" == true ]]; then
   702→    echo "Run './scripts/generate-helm-values.sh $ENV' to regenerate." >&2
   703→    exit 1
   704→  fi
   705→  exit 0
   706→fi
   707→
   708→# --- Write outputs ---
   709→mkdir -p "$(dirname "$UPSTREAM_FILE")"
   710→mkdir -p "$(dirname "$GATEWAY_FILE")"
   711→echo "$UPSTREAM_GENERATED" > "$UPSTREAM_FILE"
   712→echo "$GATEWAY_GENERATED" > "$GATEWAY_FILE"
   713→echo "Generated: $UPSTREAM_FILE"
   714→echo "Generated: $GATEWAY_FILE"
   715→
```

> AGENT

When we recreated the `public` schema, the IAM user grants were lost. The database init job in Terraform re-establishes them, but it's triggered via CI with `run_database_init_job=true`.

The fastest fix right now is to run the grants manually. You'll need to set a temp password on postgres again, run the grants, then randomize it:

**1. Set temp password:**
```bash
gcloud sql users set-password postgres \
  --instance=kd-ix-eur-dev-cloudsql-platform \
  --project=kd-ix-eur-dev-platform-data \
  --password='TempGrant2026!'
```

**2. Run a psql pod and grant privileges:**
```bash
kubectl run psql-grants --rm -it \
  --namespace=crewai-platform \
  --image=postgres:15-alpine \
  --restart=Never \
  --env="PGHOST=cloud-sql-proxy" \
  --env="PGPORT=5432" \
  --env="PGUSER=postgres" \
  --env="PGPASSWORD=TempGrant2026!" \
  -- /bin/sh -c '
for DB in crewai_plus_production crewai_plus_cable_production crewai_plus_oauth_db; do
  echo "Granting privileges on $DB..."
  psql -d "$DB" -c "GRANT ALL PRIVILEGES ON SCHEMA public TO \"crewai-platform@kd-ix-eur-dev-gke.iam\";"
  psql -d "$DB" -c "GRANT ALL PRIVILEGES ON ALL TABLES IN SCHEMA public TO \"crewai-platform@kd-ix-eur-dev-gke.iam\";"
  psql -d "$DB" -c "GRANT ALL PRIVILEGES ON ALL SEQUENCES IN SCHEMA public TO \"crewai-platform@kd-ix-eur-dev-gke.iam\";"
  psql -d "$DB" -c "ALTER DEFAULT PRIVILEGES IN SCHEMA public GRANT ALL PRIVILEGES ON TABLES TO \"crewai-platform@kd-ix-eur-dev-gke.iam\";"
  psql -d "$DB" -c "ALTER DEFAULT PRIVILEGES IN SCHEMA public GRANT ALL PRIVILEGES ON SEQUENCES TO \"crewai-platform@kd-ix-eur-dev-gke.iam\";"
done
echo "Done!"
'
```

**3. Randomize the password again:**
```bash
gcloud sql users set-password postgres \
  --instance=kd-ix-eur-dev-cloudsql-platform \
  --project=kd-ix-eur-dev-platform-data \
  --password="$(openssl rand -base64 32)"
```

**4. Restart deployments to re-trigger migrations:**
```bash
kubectl rollout restart deployment -n crewai-platform
```

> DEVELOPER

isnt that what helm job crewai-db-permissions does?

> AGENT

You're right - the gateway chart already has a `databasePermissions` job configured with the IAM user and all three databases. That should handle the grants.

You can re-trigger it by deleting the completed job so ArgoCD recreates it:

```bash
kubectl delete job crewai-db-permissions -n crewai-platform
```

Then either let ArgoCD auto-sync pick it up, or force a sync:

```bash
argocd app sync crewai-dev
```

That job should re-run the same privilege grants against all three databases without needing to touch the postgres password.

> AGENT

The Helm job connects as `postgres` but the password we randomized earlier is blocking it. Note that the job is also not checking exit codes - it prints "Permissions granted" despite every psql command failing. So the grants are **not** being applied.

You'll need to do this manually. Set a temp password, run the grants, then randomize it:

```bash
# 1. Set temp password
gcloud sql users set-password postgres \
  --instance=kd-ix-eur-dev-cloudsql-platform \
  --project=kd-ix-eur-dev-platform-data \
  --password='TempGrant2026!'

# 2. Run grants
kubectl run psql-grants --rm -it \
  --namespace=crewai-platform \
  --image=postgres:15-alpine \
  --restart=Never \
  --env="PGHOST=cloud-sql-proxy" \
  --env="PGPORT=5432" \
  --env="PGUSER=postgres" \
  --env="PGPASSWORD=TempGrant2026!" \
  -- /bin/sh -c '
for DB in crewai_plus_production crewai_plus_cable_production crewai_plus_oauth_db; do
  echo "Granting on $DB..."
  psql -d "$DB" -c "GRANT ALL PRIVILEGES ON SCHEMA public TO \"crewai-platform@kd-ix-eur-dev-gke.iam\";"
  psql -d "$DB" -c "ALTER DEFAULT PRIVILEGES IN SCHEMA public GRANT ALL PRIVILEGES ON TABLES TO \"crewai-platform@kd-ix-eur-dev-gke.iam\";"
  psql -d "$DB" -c "ALTER DEFAULT PRIVILEGES IN SCHEMA public GRANT ALL PRIVILEGES ON SEQUENCES TO \"crewai-platform@kd-ix-eur-dev-gke.iam\";"
done
echo "Done!"
'

# 3. Randomize password
gcloud sql users set-password postgres \
  --instance=kd-ix-eur-dev-cloudsql-platform \
  --project=kd-ix-eur-dev-platform-data \
  --password="$(openssl rand -base64 32)"

# 4. Restart to trigger migrations
kubectl rollout restart deployment -n crewai-platform
```

Separately, the `crewai-db-permissions` Helm job has a bug - it doesn't fail on psql errors. That's worth fixing later so it doesn't silently report success.

> DEVELOPER

lets fix that issue with it not failing, we shouldnt be doing any kubectl jobs to fix it! i did a terraform destroy and re-build, so why is it failing? is there a database password mismatch?

> AGENT

Good question. The Cloud SQL **instance** (`kd-ix-eur-dev-cloudsql-platform`) is a data source, not managed by this Terraform. So `terraform destroy` and rebuild recreated the databases and IAM user, but the instance still has the randomized postgres password we set earlier.

Let me check how the Helm job gets the postgres password.

```
     1→{{- /*
     2→Database permissions job - runs as PreSync to ensure IAM user has schema access.
     3→Must run before migration jobs that need CREATE TABLE permissions.
     4→
     5→Uses postgres superuser to grant permissions to the IAM service account.
     6→*/ -}}
     7→{{- if .Values.databaseConfig.enabled }}
     8→apiVersion: batch/v1
     9→kind: Job
    10→metadata:
    11→  name: {{ include "crewai-gateway.fullname" . }}-db-permissions
    12→  namespace: {{ .Release.Namespace }}
    13→  labels:
    14→    {{- include "crewai-gateway.labels" . | nindent 4 }}
    15→    app.kubernetes.io/component: db-permissions
    16→  annotations:
    17→    argocd.argoproj.io/hook: PreSync
    18→    argocd.argoproj.io/sync-wave: "-20"
    19→    argocd.argoproj.io/hook-delete-policy: BeforeHookCreation
    20→    description: "Grants schema permissions to IAM user for Rails migrations"
    21→spec:
    22→  ttlSecondsAfterFinished: 300
    23→  backoffLimit: 3
    24→  template:
    25→    metadata:
    26→      labels:
    27→        {{- include "crewai-gateway.labels" . | nindent 8 }}
    28→        app.kubernetes.io/component: db-permissions
    29→    spec:
    30→      restartPolicy: Never
    31→      containers:
    32→        - name: psql
    33→          image: postgres:15-alpine
    34→          env:
    35→            - name: PGHOST
    36→              value: "cloud-sql-proxy"
    37→            - name: PGUSER
    38→              value: "postgres"
    39→            - name: PGPASSWORD
    40→              valueFrom:
    41→                secretKeyRef:
    42→                  name: {{ .Values.databasePermissions.postgresSecretName | default "postgres-credentials" }}
    43→                  key: {{ .Values.databasePermissions.postgresSecretKey | default "password" }}
    44→            - name: IAM_USER
    45→              value: {{ .Values.databasePermissions.iamUser | required "databasePermissions.iamUser is required" | quote }}
    46→            - name: DB_PRODUCTION
    47→              value: {{ .Values.databasePermissions.databases.production | default "crewai_plus_production" | quote }}
    48→            - name: DB_CABLE
    49→              value: {{ .Values.databasePermissions.databases.cable | default "crewai_plus_cable_production" | quote }}
    50→            - name: DB_OAUTH
    51→              value: {{ .Values.databasePermissions.databases.oauth | default "crewai_plus_oauth_db" | quote }}
    52→          command: ["/bin/sh", "-c"]
    53→          args:
    54→            - |
    55→              set -e
    56→              echo "=============================================="
    57→              echo "Database Permissions Setup"
    58→              echo "=============================================="
    59→              echo "IAM User: $IAM_USER"
    60→              echo "Databases: $DB_PRODUCTION, $DB_CABLE, $DB_OAUTH"
    61→              echo "----------------------------------------------"
    62→
    63→              for DB in "$DB_PRODUCTION" "$DB_CABLE" "$DB_OAUTH"; do
    64→                echo ""
    65→                echo "[DB: $DB] Creating extensions..."
    66→                psql -d "$DB" -c "
    67→                  CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS pgcrypto;
    68→                  CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS citext;
    69→                  CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS hstore;
    70→                  CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS pg_trgm;
    71→                " 2>&1 | grep -v "already exists" || true
    72→                echo "[DB: $DB] Extensions ready"
    73→
    74→                echo "[DB: $DB] Reassigning object ownership to postgres..."
    75→                psql -d "$DB" -c "
    76→                  -- Reassign all objects owned by IAM user to postgres so we can grant
    77→                  REASSIGN OWNED BY \"$IAM_USER\" TO postgres;
    78→                " 2>&1 | grep -v "^$" || true
    79→                echo "[DB: $DB] Ownership reassigned"
    80→
    81→                echo "[DB: $DB] Granting schema permissions..."
    82→                psql -d "$DB" -c "
    83→                  -- Grant schema access
    84→                  GRANT ALL ON SCHEMA public TO \"$IAM_USER\";
    85→
    86→                  -- Grant on existing objects
    87→                  GRANT ALL PRIVILEGES ON ALL TABLES IN SCHEMA public TO \"$IAM_USER\";
    88→                  GRANT ALL PRIVILEGES ON ALL SEQUENCES IN SCHEMA public TO \"$IAM_USER\";
    89→                  GRANT ALL PRIVILEGES ON ALL FUNCTIONS IN SCHEMA public TO \"$IAM_USER\";
    90→
    91→                  -- Grant on future objects
    92→                  ALTER DEFAULT PRIVILEGES IN SCHEMA public GRANT ALL ON TABLES TO \"$IAM_USER\";
    93→                  ALTER DEFAULT PRIVILEGES IN SCHEMA public GRANT ALL ON SEQUENCES TO \"$IAM_USER\";
    94→                  ALTER DEFAULT PRIVILEGES IN SCHEMA public GRANT ALL ON FUNCTIONS TO \"$IAM_USER\";
    95→                " 2>&1 | grep -v "^$" || true
    96→                echo "[DB: $DB] Permissions granted to $IAM_USER"
    97→              done
    98→
    99→              echo ""
   100→              echo "=============================================="
   101→              echo "SUCCESS: All databases configured"
   102→              echo "=============================================="
   103→{{- end }}
   104→
```

```
     1→{{- /*

     3→Used by db-permissions job to grant schema access to IAM users.
     4→Runs at wave -25 (before db-permissions at -20).
     5→Uses the platform-managed ClusterSecretStore (gcp-secret-manager).
     6→*/ -}}
     7→{{- if .Values.databaseConfig.enabled }}
     8→apiVersion: external-secrets.io/v1beta1
     9→kind: ExternalSecret
    10→metadata:
    11→  name: {{ include "crewai-gateway.fullname" . }}-postgres
    12→  namespace: {{ .Release.Namespace }}
    13→  labels:
    14→    {{- include "crewai-gateway.labels" . | nindent 4 }}
    15→  annotations:
    16→    argocd.argoproj.io/hook: PreSync
    17→    argocd.argoproj.io/sync-wave: "-25"
    18→spec:
    19→  refreshInterval: 1h
    20→  secretStoreRef:
    21→    name: gcp-secret-manager
    22→    kind: ClusterSecretStore
    23→  target:
    24→    name: {{ .Values.databasePermissions.postgresSecretName | default "postgres-credentials" }}
    25→    creationPolicy: Owner
    26→    template:
    27→      type: Opaque
    28→      metadata:
    29→        labels:
    30→          {{- include "crewai-gateway.labels" . | nindent 10 }}
    31→  data:
    32→    - secretKey: {{ .Values.databasePermissions.postgresSecretKey | default "password" }}
    33→      remoteRef:
    34→        key: {{ .Values.databasePermissions.postgresSecretGcpName | default "cloudsql-postgres-password" }}
    35→{{- end }}
    36→
```

> AGENT

Now I see the full picture. Here's the chain:

1. **ExternalSecret** (`externalsecret-postgres.yaml`, wave -25) pulls the postgres password from GCP Secret Manager secret `cloudsql-postgres-password` in `kd-ix-eur-dev-security`
2. **Job** (`job-db-permissions.yaml`, wave -20) uses that password to connect as postgres and run grants

**Two problems:**

**Problem 1 - Password mismatch:** We randomized the postgres password on the Cloud SQL instance, but the `cloudsql-postgres-password` GCP Secret Manager secret still has the old value. They're out of sync. This Terraform doesn't manage that secret - it's probably in `kix-platform-infrastructure`.

Fix: set the instance password to match what's in Secret Manager:

```bash
# Read what Secret Manager thinks the password is
gcloud secrets versions access latest \
  --secret=[REDACTED] \
  --project=kd-ix-eur-dev-security

# Set the Cloud SQL instance password to match
gcloud sql users set-password postgres \
  --instance=kd-ix-eur-dev-cloudsql-platform \
  --project=kd-ix-eur-dev-platform-data \
  --password='<value from above>'
```

**Problem 2 - Silent failures in the job script:** The `|| true` on lines 71, 78, and 95 of [job-db-permissions.yaml](environments/dev/helm/crewai-gateway/templates/job-db-permissions.yaml) masks psql failures despite `set -e`. Each psql command pipes through `grep -v ... || true`, so the exit code is always 0.

Want me to fix the job template to properly fail on psql errors?

> AGENT

You can do it in one shot by piping the Secret Manager value directly:

```bash
gcloud sql users set-password postgres \
  --instance=kd-ix-eur-dev-cloudsql-platform \
  --project=kd-ix-eur-dev-platform-data \
  --password="$(gcloud secrets versions access latest --secret=[REDACTED] --project=kd-ix-eur-dev-security)"
```

Then re-sync to re-trigger the db-permissions job:

```bash
kubectl delete job -n crewai-platform -l app.kubernetes.io/component=db-permissions
argocd app sync crewai-dev
```

> DEVELOPER

now fix the silent failures


> AGENT

The issue is the `2>&1 | grep -v ... || true` pattern on lines 71, 78, and 95. The pipe means `set -e` only checks the exit code of the last command in the pipeline (`grep` or `true`), not `psql`. And `|| true` ensures it never fails.

> TOOL

> AGENT

Changes made:

- **`set -e`** → **`set -eo pipefail`** — ensures failures in piped commands are caught
- Removed all `2>&1 | grep -v ... || true` patterns — psql errors now propagate and fail the job
- Removed the SQL comment lines (unnecessary noise in the output)

Now if psql can't connect or any grant fails, the job will fail with a non-zero exit code, and ArgoCD's PreSync hook will block the sync instead of silently proceeding.
