> AGENT

Done. PR [#226](https://github.com/konecta-ix-services/kix-services-crewai/pull/226) merged.

**What this does:** After each ArgoCD sync, a PostSync Job patches the `docker-registry` secret to add a `config.json` key alongside the existing `.dockerconfigjson` key. Since the secret is mounted without `items` filtering, both files appear in `/root/.docker/`, and BuildKit will find `config.json` with the registry credentials.

Note: the current `docker-registry` secret only has auth for `images.crewai.com` and `registry.crewai.com` (CrewAI registries), **not** for `europe-west1-docker.pkg.dev` (Artifact Registry). This will fix BuildKit's ability to find *any* credentials, but AR pulls will still need auth. Since the Workload Identity token works (we proved that earlier), the next question is whether the CrewAI app passes AR credentials separately when invoking BuildKit, or if we also need to add AR auth to the docker config.

> DEVELOPER

ok, once synced, whats the quickest way to test it?

> AGENT

Once ArgoCD syncs, run these in order:

```bash
# 1. Verify the PostSync job ran
kubectl get jobs -n crewai | grep fix-docker

# 2. Check the secret now has both keys
kubectl get secret docker-registry -n crewai -o jsonpath='{.data}' | python3 -c "import sys,json; print(list(json.load(sys.stdin).keys()))"
# Should show: ['.dockerconfigjson', 'config.json']

# 3. Check BuildKit can see config.json (may take ~60s for mount refresh)
kubectl exec deploy/crewai-buildkit -n crewai -- ls -la /root/.docker/config.json

# 4. Trigger a build from the web pod
kubectl exec deploy/crewai-web -n crewai -- bundle exec rake studio:agent:install
```

If step 3 still shows "No such file", the BuildKit pod may need a restart to pick up the new secret key:

```bash
kubectl rollout restart deployment/crewai-buildkit -n crewai
```

```
# BuildKit Chart Values - Authentication & Service

Based on the documentation provided, here are the available BuildKit chart values related to registry authentication and credentials:

## Registry Authentication

**`buildkit.dockerConfigSecret`** (string, default: `"docker-registry"`)
- Kubernetes secret containing Docker config for registry access
- Should include a `.dockerconfigjson` key with base64-encoded credentials
- Example: Create via `kubectl create secret docker-registry` with server, username, and password parameters

## Registry Configuration

**`buildkit.registries`** (array, default: `[]`)
- Configures registry mirrors and insecure registries
- Supports fields: `hostname`, `http`, `insecure`, and `mirrors`
- Enables scenarios like internal registry setup, pull-through mirrors, and air-gapped environments

## Image Pull Credentials

**`buildkit.image.pullSecret`** (string, default: `""`)
- Specifies image pull secret for BuildKit container
- Falls back to `image.pullSecret` when empty

## Service Account & Identity

The documentation does not mention service account configuration, workload identity, GCP-specific settings, or credential helpers for BuildKit deployment.
```

> AGENT

The `config.json` is there now but it only has CrewAI registry auth — no entry for `europe-west1-docker.pkg.dev`. The chart doesn't have built-in Workload Identity support for BuildKit.

Since we proved the Workload Identity token works from inside the pod, the fix is to update our PostSync job to also fetch an access token from the metadata server and add AR auth to the docker config. Let me update the job.

```
     1→{{- /*
     2→Patches the docker-registry secret to add a "config.json" key.
     3→The upstream chart creates this as kubernetes.io/dockerconfigjson type
     4→which uses the key ".dockerconfigjson", but BuildKit reads
     5→$HOME/.docker/config.json and cannot find it under that key name.
     6→
     7→This job copies the .dockerconfigjson data into a config.json key
     8→so BuildKit can discover the registry credentials.
     9→*/ -}}
    10→apiVersion: batch/v1
    11→kind: Job
    12→metadata:
    13→  name: {{ include "crewai-gateway.fullname" . }}-fix-docker-registry
    14→  namespace: {{ .Release.Namespace }}
    15→  labels:
    16→    {{- include "crewai-gateway.labels" . | nindent 4 }}
    17→    app.kubernetes.io/component: fix-docker-registry
    18→  annotations:
    19→    argocd.argoproj.io/hook: PostSync
    20→    argocd.argoproj.io/hook-delete-policy: BeforeHookCreation
    21→    description: "Patches docker-registry secret to add config.json key for BuildKit"
    22→spec:
    23→  ttlSecondsAfterFinished: 120
    24→  backoffLimit: 3
    25→  template:
    26→    metadata:
    27→      labels:
    28→        {{- include "crewai-gateway.labels" . | nindent 8 }}
    29→        app.kubernetes.io/component: fix-docker-registry
    30→    spec:
    31→      restartPolicy: Never
    32→      serviceAccountName: {{ .Values.crewai.serviceAccountName | default "crewai-sa" }}
    33→      containers:
    34→        - name: patch
    35→          image: bitnami/kubectl:latest
    36→          command: ["/bin/sh", "-c"]
    37→          args:
    38→            - |
    39→              set -eo pipefail
    40→              echo "Patching docker-registry secret to add config.json key..."
    41→
    42→              # Read the existing .dockerconfigjson value (base64-encoded in the secret)
    43→              DATA=$(kubectl get secret docker-registry -n {{ .Release.Namespace }} \
    44→                -o jsonpath='{.data.\.dockerconfigjson}')
    45→
    46→              if [ -z "$DATA" ]; then
    47→                echo "ERROR: docker-registry secret not found or .dockerconfigjson key is empty"
    48→                exit 1
    49→              fi
    50→
    51→              # Patch the secret to add config.json key with the same data
    52→              kubectl patch secret docker-registry -n {{ .Release.Namespace }} \
    53→                --type='json' \
    54→                -p="[{\"op\":\"add\",\"path\":\"/data/config.json\",\"value\":\"${DATA}\"}]"
    55→
    56→              echo "SUCCESS: config.json key added to docker-registry secret"
    57→              echo "BuildKit will find credentials at /root/.docker/config.json on next restart"
    58→
```

```json
{
  "file_path": "/home/nathan_booth/code/konecta/konecta-tic/kix-services-crewai/environments/dev/helm/crewai-gateway/templates/job-fix-docker-registry.yaml",
  "content": "{{- /*\nPatches the docker-registry secret to add a \"config.json\" key with\nArtifact Registry auth via Workload Identity.\n\nThe upstream chart creates the secret as kubernetes.io/dockerconfigjson\ntype (key: .dockerconfigjson), but BuildKit reads $HOME/.docker/config.json.\nAdditionally, the upstream secret only has CrewAI registry auth — it has no\ncredentials for GCP Artifact Registry.\n\nThis job:\n1. Reads the existing .dockerconfigjson (CrewAI registry auth)\n2. Gets an access token from the GKE metadata server (Workload Identity)\n3. Merges AR auth into the config\n4. Patches the secret with a config.json key containing both\n*/ -}}\napiVersion: batch/v1\nkind: Job\nmetadata:\n  name: {{ include \"crewai-gateway.fullname\" . }}-fix-docker-registry\n  namespace: {{ .Release.Namespace }}\n  labels:\n    {{- include \"crewai-gateway.labels\" . | nindent 4 }}\n    app.kubernetes.io/component: fix-docker-registry\n  annotations:\n    argocd.argoproj.io/hook: PostSync\n    argocd.argoproj.io/hook-delete-policy: BeforeHookCreation\n    description: \"Patches docker-registry secret with config.json and AR auth for BuildKit\"\nspec:\n  ttlSecondsAfterFinished: 120\n  backoffLimit: 3\n  template:\n    metadata:\n      labels:\n        {{- include \"crewai-gateway.labels\" . | nindent 8 }}\n        app.kubernetes.io/component: fix-docker-registry\n    spec:\n      restartPolicy: Never\n      serviceAccountName: {{ .Values.crewai.serviceAccountName | default \"crewai-sa\" }}\n      containers:\n        - name: patch\n          image: bitnami/kubectl:latest\n          command: [\"/bin/sh\", \"-c\"]\n          args:\n            - |\n              set -eo pipefail\n              echo \"==============================================\"\n              echo \"Patching docker-registry secret for BuildKit\"\n              echo \"==============================================\"\n\n              # 1. Read existing docker config\n              EXISTING_B64=$(kubectl get secret docker-registry -n {{ .Release.Namespace }} \\\n                -o jsonpath='{.data.\\.dockerconfigjson}')\n\n              if [ -z \"$EXISTING_B64\" ]; then\n                echo \"ERROR: docker-registry secret not found or empty\"\n                exit 1\n              fi\n\n              EXISTING=$(echo \"$EXISTING_B64\" | base64 -d)\n              echo \"Existing registries: $(echo \"$EXISTING\" | python3 -c \"import sys,json; print(list(json.load(sys.stdin).get('auths',{}).keys()))\" 2>/dev/null || echo \"unknown\")\"\n\n              # 2. Get Workload Identity access token from metadata server\n              echo \"Fetching Workload Identity access token...\"\n              TOKEN=$(wget -qO- \\\n                --header=\"Metadata-Flavor: Google\" \\\n                \"http://metadata.google.internal/computeMetadata/v1/instance/service-accounts/default/token\" \\\n                2>/dev/null | python3 -c \"import sys,json; print(json.load(sys.stdin)['access_token'])\")\n\n              if [ -z \"$TOKEN\" ]; then\n                echo \"WARNING: Could not get Workload Identity token - patching without AR auth\"\n                MERGED=\"$EXISTING\"\n              else\n                echo \"Access token retrieved (length: ${#TOKEN})\"\n\n                # 3. Merge AR auth into existing config\n                MERGED=$(echo \"$EXISTING\" | python3 -c \"\nimport sys, json, base64\nconfig = json.load(sys.stdin)\ntoken = '$TOKEN'\nauth = base64.b64encode(f'oauth2accesstoken:{token}'.encode()).decode()\nconfig.setdefault('auths', {})['europe-west1-docker.pkg.dev'] = {'auth': auth}\nprint(json.dumps(config))\n\")\n                echo \"Merged registries: $(echo \"$MERGED\" | python3 -c \"import sys,json; print(list(json.load(sys.stdin).get('auths',{}).keys()))\")\"\n              fi\n\n              # 4. Patch secret with config.json key\n              MERGED_B64=$(echo -n \"$MERGED\" | base64 -w 0)\n\n              kubectl patch secret docker-registry -n {{ .Release.Namespace }} \\\n                --type='json' \\\n                -p=\"[{\\\"op\\\":\\\"add\\\",\\\"path\\\":\\\"/data/config.json\\\",\\\"value\\\":\\\"${MERGED_B64}\\\"}]\"\n\n              echo \"\"\n              echo \"==============================================\"\n              echo \"SUCCESS: config.json patched with AR auth\"\n              echo \"==============================================\"\n"
}
```

After ArgoCD syncs, test with:

```bash
# 1. Check PostSync job ran
kubectl get jobs -n crewai | grep fix-docker

# 2. Verify config.json now has all 3 registries
kubectl exec deploy/crewai-buildkit -n crewai -- \
  cat /root/.docker/config.json | python3 -c "import sys,json; print(list(json.load(sys.stdin)['auths'].keys()))"
# Should show: ['images.crewai.com', 'registry.crewai.com', 'europe-west1-docker.pkg.dev']
```

Note: the Workload Identity access token expires after ~1 hour. Each ArgoCD sync refreshes it via the PostSync hook. If builds fail after a long period without sync, a manual sync or a CronJob to refresh the token would be needed.

```
---
# Source: crewai-gateway/templates/job-fix-docker-registry.yaml
apiVersion: batch/v1
kind: Job
metadata:
  name: crewai-fix-docker-registry
  namespace: default
  labels:
    helm.sh/chart: crewai-gateway-0.1.0
    app.kubernetes.io/name: crewai-gateway
    app.kubernetes.io/instance: crewai
    app.kubernetes.io/version: "1.0.0"
    app.kubernetes.io/managed-by: Helm
    environment: dev
    app.kubernetes.io/component: fix-docker-registry
  annotations:
    argocd.argoproj.io/hook: PostSync
    argocd.argoproj.io/hook-delete-policy: BeforeHookCreation
    description: "Patches docker-registry secret with config.json and AR auth for BuildKit"
spec:
  ttlSecondsAfterFinished: 120
  backoffLimit: 3
  template:
    metadata:
      labels:
        helm.sh/chart: crewai-gateway-0.1.0
        app.kubernetes.io/name: crewai-gateway
        app.kubernetes.io/instance: crewai
        app.kubernetes.io/version: "1.0.0"
        app.kubernetes.io/managed-by: Helm
        environment: dev
        app.kubernetes.io/component: fix-docker-registry
    spec:
      restartPolicy: Never
      serviceAccountName: crewai-sa
      containers:
        - name: patch
          image: bitnami/kubectl:latest
          command: ["/bin/sh", "-c"]
          args:
            - |
              set -eo pipefail
              echo "=============================================="
              echo "Patching docker-registry secret for BuildKit"
              echo "=============================================="

              # 1. Read existing docker config
              EXISTING_B64=$(kubectl get secret docker-registry -n default \
                -o jsonpath='{.data.\.dockerconfigjson}')

              if [ -z "$EXISTING_B64" ]; then
                echo "ERROR: docker-registry secret not found or empty"
                exit 1
              fi

              EXISTING=$(echo "$EXISTING_B64" | base64 -d)
              echo "Existing registries: $(echo "$EXISTING" | python3 -c "import sys,json; print(list(json.load(sys.stdin).get('auths',{}).keys()))" 2>/dev/null || echo "unknown")"

              # 2. Get Workload Identity access token from metadata server
              echo "Fetching Workload Identity access token..."
              TOKEN=$(wget -qO- \
                --header="Metadata-Flavor: Google" \
                "http://metadata.google.internal/computeMetadata/v1/instance/service-accounts/default/token" \
                2>/dev/null | python3 -c "import sys,json; print(json.load(sys.stdin)['access_token'])")

              if [ -z "$TOKEN" ]; then
                echo "WARNING: Could not get Workload Identity token - patching without AR auth"
                MERGED="$EXISTING"
              else
                echo "Access token retrieved (length: ${#TOKEN})"

                # 3. Merge AR auth into existing config
                MERGED=$(echo "$EXISTING" | python3 -c "import sys,json,base64; c=json.load(sys.stdin); a=base64.b64encode(('oauth2accesstoken:'+sys.argv[1]).encode()).decode(); c.setdefault('auths',{})['europe-west1-docker.pkg.dev']={'auth':a}; print(json.dumps(c))" "$TOKEN")
                echo "Merged registries: $(echo "$MERGED" | python3 -c "import sys,json; print(list(json.load(sys.stdin).get('auths',{}).keys()))")"
              fi

              # 4. Patch secret with config.json key
              MERGED_B64=$(echo -n "$MERGED" | base64 -w 0)

              kubectl patch secret docker-registry -n default \
                --type='json' \
                -p="[{\"op\":\"add\",\"path\":\"/data/config.json\",\"value\":\"${MERGED_B64}\"}]"

              echo ""
              echo "=============================================="
              echo "SUCCESS: config.json patched with AR auth"
              echo "=============================================="
```

Reads the existing .dockerconfigjson (CrewAI registry auth)\n2. Gets an access token from the GKE metadata server (Workload Identity)\n3. Merges AR auth into the config\n4. Patches the secret with a config.json key containing both\n\nUses only shell builtins, wget, and base64 (no python3/jq dependency).\n*/ -}}\napiVersion: batch/v1\nkind: Job\nmetadata:\n  name: {{ include \"crewai-gateway.fullname\" . }}-fix-docker-registry\n  namespace: {{ .Release.Namespace }}\n  labels:\n    {{- include \"crewai-gateway.labels\" . | nindent 4 }}\n    app.kubernetes.io/component: fix-docker-registry\n  annotations:\n    argocd.argoproj.io/hook: PostSync\n    argocd.argoproj.io/hook-delete-policy: BeforeHookCreation\n    description: \"Patches docker-registry secret with config.json and AR auth for BuildKit\"\nspec:\n  ttlSecondsAfterFinished: 120\n  backoffLimit: 3\n  template:\n    metadata:\n      labels:\n        {{- include \"crewai-gateway.labels\" . | nindent 8 }}\n        app.kubernetes.io/component: fix-docker-registry\n    spec:\n      restartPolicy: Never\n      serviceAccountName: {{ .Values.crewai.serviceAccountName | default \"crewai-sa\" }}\n      containers:\n        - name: patch\n          image: bitnami/kubectl:latest\n          command: [\"/bin/sh\", \"-c\"]\n          args:\n            - |\n              set -eo pipefail\n              echo \"==============================================\"\n              echo \"Patching docker-registry secret for BuildKit\"\n              echo \"==============================================\"\n\n              # 1. Read existing docker config\n              EXISTING_B64=$(kubectl get secret docker-registry -n {{ .Release.Namespace }} \\\n                -o jsonpath='{.data.\\.dockerconfigjson}')\n\n              if [ -z \"$EXISTING_B64\" ]; then\n                echo \"ERROR: docker-registry secret not found or empty\"\n                exit 1\n              fi\n\n              EXISTING=$(echo \"$EXISTING_B64\" | base64 -d)\n              echo \"Existing config loaded (${#EXISTING} bytes)\"\n\n              # 2. Get Workload Identity access token from metadata server\n              echo \"Fetching Workload Identity access token...\"\n              TOKEN_JSON=$(wget -qO- \\\n                --header=\"Metadata-Flavor: Google\" \\\n                \"http://metadata.google.internal/computeMetadata/v1/instance/service-accounts/default/token\" \\\n                2>/dev/null || echo \"\")\n\n              # Extract access_token value using sed (no python/jq needed)\n              TOKEN=$(echo \"$TOKEN_JSON\" | sed -n 's/.*\"access_token\":\"\\([^\"]*\\)\".*/\\1/p')\n\n              if [ -z \"$TOKEN\" ]; then\n                echo \"WARNING: Could not get Workload Identity token - patching without AR auth\"\n                MERGED=\"$EXISTING\"\n              else\n                echo \"Access token retrieved (length: ${#TOKEN})\"\n\n                # 3. Build AR auth entry: base64(\"oauth2accesstoken:<token>\")\n                AR_AUTH=$(printf 'oauth2accesstoken:%s' \"$TOKEN\" | base64 | tr -d '\\n')\n\n                # 4. Merge AR auth into existing config\n                # Insert AR entry before the closing }}\n                # Existing format: {\"auths\":{\"reg1\":{...},\"reg2\":{...}}}\n                MERGED=$(echo \"$EXISTING\" | sed \"s|\\\"}}$|\\\"},\\\"europe-west1-docker.pkg.dev\\\":{\\\"auth\\\":\\\"${AR_AUTH}\\\"}}}|\")\n                echo \"AR auth merged into config\"\n              fi\n\n              # 5. Patch secret with config.json key\n              MERGED_B64=$(echo -n \"$MERGED\" | base64 | tr -d '\\n')\n\n              kubectl patch secret docker-registry -n {{ .Release.Namespace }} \\\n                --type='json' \\\n                -p=\"[{\\\"op\\\":\\\"add\\\",\\\"path\\\":\\\"/data/config.json\\\",\\\"value\\\":\\\"${MERGED_B64}\\\"}]\"\n\n              echo \"\"\n              echo \"==============================================\"\n              echo \"SUCCESS: config.json patched with AR auth\"\n              echo \"==============================================\"\n"
```json
}
```
